update-setup-sheet-start: RED RUN (task 2.5) - written 2026-10-10, before any change in src/.
Command: npm run test:run   (vitest, jsdom)   Baseline before section 2: 56 files, 1252 tests, all passing.
Result:  58 files (29 failed, 29 passed), 1293 tests: 217 FAILED, 1076 passed.   (+2 new files, +41 tests net.)

Why they fail (all 217 are AssertionError; no SyntaxError, TypeError, import or compile error; tsc --noEmit and eslint are clean):
  174 + 1  "expected an element matching [data-action="setup-start"]" / "exactly one [data-action="setup-start"] in the sheet":
           the sheet has no «Почати» yet. This is every test that chooses (mark + «Почати») through markSize/markLevel + pressStart,
           selectSize or chooseLevel, so it includes the helper-only (kind A) tests and the call-site edits (kind B).
  42       assertion mismatches that state the new rule: a marking press still starts a puzzle (the board, the summary, a dialog,
           hidePopover, seeds change), aria-checked still follows the board shown, the sheet has 3 children and 8 buttons (not 4 and 9),
           52 buttons (not 53), no 'Почати' entry in strings.ts, no 44 px / colour rule for the start button.

Cross-check that the failures are reachable-green (not impossible tests): the same 1293 tests PASS (1293/1293) against a throw-away
prototype of the change kept OUTSIDE the worktree (scratchpad copy; src/ of this worktree was not touched), and the 45 Playwright tests PASS
against it; 9 deliberate mutations of the prototype (marking 4 keeps the level, «Почати» a no-op when the choice equals the board, no reset
on the closing toggle, marks not reset at «Почати», no hidePopover, no confirmation, a marking press moves focus, the pending pair uses the
shown level, an unavailable level marks) each turn 1 to 40 tests of the 8 key files red.

Guards: tests that PASS before the change (the page already does what they say). A guard is not evidence that the feature works.

PASSING NEW OR CHANGED TESTS (guards and helper self-tests; the helper self-tests run on a hand-made fake, by design):
  play-page-helpers.test.ts :: dispatchToggle(el, "closed") clears the stub open state and dispatchToggle(el, "open") sets it
  play-page-helpers.test.ts :: markSize and markLevel open the sheet only while the stub is closed, move aria-checked and never press «Почати»
  play-page-helpers.test.ts :: a mark helper reopens a sheet that was closed by hidePopover or by a closing toggle
  play-page-helpers.test.ts :: pressStart clicks [data-action="setup-start"] and nothing else
  play-page-helpers.test.ts :: pressStart fails with a readable message when the sheet has no start button
  play-page-helpers.test.ts :: selectSize and chooseLevel mark, press «Почати», and confirm exactly when the board has an entry (also for the size shown)
  play-page-marked-choice.test.ts :: With no board shown the sheet opens on the mount values

Other guards that stay green by design (changed-tests.md section 10, unchanged on purpose): the structure/default tests of the size, level and
summary controls, 'An unavailable level leaves the sheet open' (setup-sheet), 'Pressing an unavailable level changes nothing' (level-4x4), the
stylesheet scans (13 tokens, no !important, one :has(, no removed outline), 'no file of src/ui/ other than strings.ts holds Cyrillic', the
ids-stay-four tests, 'Seed is not shown'.

/** Accessible name of a cell: «Рядок R, стовпець C, V» plus «, задано» or «, підказка» (row and col are 1-based). */
export function cellLabel(row: number, col: number, value: 0 | 1 | null, given: boolean, hinted: boolean): string {
